| Some ten yrsago, my learned Professor Mahamahopadhyaya Pandita

thahara Dvivedi of the Sanskrit College, Benares, presented the Sans-
। reading public with his excellent edition of Varaha Mihira’s admi-
Je production on natural astrology —the Brihatsamhita with the

mmentary of Bhattotpala.

| The Adbhutasagara of Vallala Sena Deva (and his son Lakshmana
ta who completed the work left unfinished by his father) treats of the
ne subject, but is richer in matter, more systematic in its treatment, and.
\bearings are more suited to the needs of the modern times.

॥
| That my author had the suitability of his subject, matter to the

quirements of Ais times if not ours, particularly in view, will appear from
ed of at length in the Brihatsamhi-

3 rejection of many particulars treat
| as useless; his enlarging upon other parts with quotations from Garga,

asishtha, Parasara and others; and his introduction of new matter from
isantaraja (on Sakuna Sastra ), ‘Susruta (on medical science ), Sali-
jtra and Pilukacharya (on Veterinary ), Vyasa and Valmiki.

| My Professor in his Ganake Tarangini mentions this interesting and
onderful production of Vallala Sena and regrets that he could not procure
complete copy of it. And well might he regret, for during the Mahome-

an period this book seems to have been all but lost.

The Sanskrit College copy of it is only a confused mass of scatt2red
paves that has defied all attempts to arrange it into a connected whole.

| After three years of laborious search, at the request of the Prabha-
came by seven incomplete MSS, some consisting of

(27 Company, I
pages at the beginning, some, of those, at the end; and others, of different

yarts of the middle of the book.

| 1 then set myself to the task of arranging the scattered parts into 2

omplete whole, a task difficult in itself, and rendered doubly so by im-
lerfect and incorrect readings ; so that I at first thought of giving it to
he press in parts that I could make out of the Confused mass. Two
rears of perseverence ‘and hard labour have at last been rewarded with
071716८6 success. The parts have, as if by magic, arranged themselves into

connected whole—and here is the ADBHUTASAGARA at last.

॥
|

यवराजवधइचेव वामे कन्यावधाय च ॥

पार्थिवस्याभिषिक्ताया देउ्यास्तत्र क्षयो भवेत्‌ ॥

ताभ्यां मन्विक्षयं विधाद्रामाभ्यां सन्तिद्षणम्‌ ॥
